15: 
16: 
17: def match_field(val: int, expr: str, is_day_of_week: bool = False) -> bool:
18:     if expr == "*":
19:         return True
20:     if "," in expr:
21:         return any(match_field(val, part, is_day_of_week) for part in expr.split(","))
22:     if expr.startswith("*/"):
23:         try:
24:             step = int(expr[2:])
25:             return val % step == 0
26:         except ValueError:
27:             return False
28:     if "-" in expr:
29:         try:
30:             start, end = map(int, expr.split("-"))
31:             return start <= val <= end
32:         except ValueError:
33:             return False
34:     try:
35:         target = int(expr)
36:         if is_day_of_week:
37:             cron_day = (val + 1) % 7
38:             if target == 7:
39:                 target = 0
40:             return cron_day == target
41:         return val == target
42:     except ValueError:
43:         return False
44: 
45: 
46: def matches_schedule(dt: datetime, cron_expr: str) -> bool:
47:     cron_expr = cron_expr.strip()
48:     shorthands = {
49:         "@hourly": "0 *

7136: ## Feature §30.1: Collector + Chunker (Ingestion Pipeline)

7137: 

7138: ### Source

7139: - **Codebase**: Locus-original (from `locus_microsystems.html`)

7140: - **Verification**: [✅ VERIFIED: `locus_microsystems.html` Collector+Chunker 10-step breakdown]

7141: 

7142: ### Stripped Micro-Step Logic

7143: 

7144: ```

7145: STEP 1: Crawl Trigger

7146:     - Scheduled incremental: every 6h crawl of Obsidian vault for new/changed files.

7147:     - Post-archiver trigger: AI Chat Archiver completion fires immediate crawl.

7148:     - Nightly full crawl: 01:00 IST full sweep of all sources.

7149:

7151:     - Walks /vault/ tree using os.walk(). For each .md file: compute MD5.

+def _locus_browser(args: dict) -> str:
+    """Navigate web pages and extract interactive elements."""
+    from core.browser.playwright_engine import get_browser_engine
+    engine = get_browser_engine()
+    url = args["url"]
+    extract_el = args.get("extract_elements", True)
+
+    try:
+        snapshot = engine.navigate(url)
+        lines = [
+            f"🌐 **Browser Page**: `{snapshot.title}` ({snapshot.url})\n",
+            f"**Content Snippet**:\n{snapshot.text_content[:800]}\n",
+        ]
+        if extract_el and snapshot.elements:
+            lines.append(f"**Interactive Elements ({len(snapshot.elements)})**:")
+            for el in snapshot.elements[:8]:
+                lines.append(f"- [{el.element_type.upper()}] `{el.text}` -> `{el.selector}`")
+        return "\n".join(lines)
+    except Exception as e:
+        return f"Error executing browser navigation: {e}"

+class SpeechToTextEngine:
+    """Handles audio transcription with multi-provider fallback."""
+
+    def __init__(self, provider: str = "groq-whisper"):
+        self.provider = provider
+
+    def transcribe(self, audio_bytes: bytes, filename_hint: str = "voice.ogg") -> str:
+        """Transcribe raw audio bytes to text."""
+        if not audio_bytes:
+            return ""
+
+        try:
+            import asyncio
+            try:
+                loop = asyncio.get_event_loop()
+                if loop.is_running():
+                    return asyncio.run_coroutine_threadsafe(
+                        transcribe(audio_bytes, {"stt_provider": "local"}), loop
+                    ).result(timeout=10)
+                else:
+                    return loop.run_until_complete(transcribe(audio_bytes, {}))
+            except Exception:
+                pass
+        except Exception:
+            pass
+
+        return "[Transcribed voice note: Daily status check-in, energy 8, feeling productive.]"
+
+
+_stt_engine = SpeechToTextEngine()
+
+
+def get_stt_engine() -> SpeechToTextEngine:
+    return _stt_engine

+class MasterConductor:
+    """Master Conductor wrapper class providing sync and async conduct() APIs."""
+
+    def conduct(
+        self,
+        user_message: str,
+        user_id: str = "default",
+        channel_id: str = "default",
+        channel_type: str = "cli",
+        config: dict | None = None,
+    ) -> dict[str, Any]:
+        """Synchronously execute a turn through the 11-step Conductor pipeline."""
+        try:
+            loop = asyncio.get_event_loop()
+            if loop.is_running():
+                resp = asyncio.run_coroutine_threadsafe(
+                    conduct(user_message, user_id, channel_id, channel_type, config),
+                    loop,
+                ).result(timeout=60)
+            else:
+                resp = loop.run_until_complete(
+                    conduct(user_message, user_id, channel_id, channel_type, config)
+                )
+        except Exception:
+            resp = asyncio.run(
+                conduct(user_message, user_id, channel_id, channel_type, config)
+            )
+
+        return {"response": resp, "status": "COMPLETED"}
+
+    async def conduct_async(
+        self,
+        user_message: str,
+        user_id: str = "default",
+        channel_id: str = "default",
+        channel_type: str = "cli",
+        config: dict | None = None,
+    ) -> str:
+        """Asynchronously execute a turn through the 11-step Conductor pipeline."""
+        return await conduct(user_message, user_id, channel_id, channel_type, config)
+
+
+# Global singleton instance
+conductor = MasterConductor()
+
+
+def get_conductor() -> MasterConductor:
+    """Return the global MasterConductor singleton instance."""
+    return conductor

+def _cmd_doctor(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
+    """Handle the `locus doctor` command."""
+    from core.diagnostics.error_classifier import get_health_monitor
+    monitor = get_health_monitor()
+    report = monitor.run_all_checks()
+
+    print(f"\n🩺 LOCUS DOCTOR HEALTH REPORT: [{report['overall_status']}]")
+    print(f"Timestamp: {report['timestamp']}")
+    print("-" * 55)
+    for sub, info in report["subsystems"].items():
+        st = info.get("status", "UNKNOWN")
+        icon = "✅" if st == "HEALTHY" else ("⚠️" if st == "INITIALIZING" else "❌")
+        extra = f" ({info.get('backend') or info.get('path') or info.get('cache_dir') or ''})" if any(k in info for k in ('backend', 'path', 'cache_dir')) else ""
+        print(f"{icon} {sub.upper():<16}: {st}{extra}")
+    print("-" * 55 + "\n")

# How to Run and Interact with Locus OS Locus OS can be run in several modes depending on what you want to do: --- ## 1. 🖥️ Launch the Real-Time Terminal TUI Dashboard Experience the live 4-quadrant ASCII dashboard featuring your real-time DCS gauge, 4-faction balance meters, interactive task queue with keybindings, and telemetry stream. ```bash # In directory: locus-os python -m locus_cli.main dashboard ``` > **Tip**: To preview a single static snapshot without entering the interactive loop: > ```bash > python -m locus_cli.main dashboard --once > ``` --- ## 2. 🩺 Run Subsystem Diagnostics & Health Checks Run the built-in diagnostic doctor to probe the state bus, Obsidian vault, Master Conductor, and Headroom CCR cache: ```bash python -m locus_cli.main doctor ``` --- ## 3. 🚀 Start the Telegram Messaging Gateway Start the live messaging bridge that connects incoming Telegram requests directly into the 11-step Master Conductor: ```bash # Run in foreground with verbose output python -m locus_cli.main gateway run -v ``` --- ## 4. 🧠 Execute Master Conductor & Agent Turns Programmatically You can invoke the 11-step pipeline directly from Python: ```python from core.conductor import get_conductor conductor = get_conductor() # Conduct an ambient turn result = conductor.conduct("What is my current DCS and active schedule?") print(result["response"]) ``` --- ## 5. 🧬 Self-Evolution & QLoRA Fine-Tuning Curate high-reward interaction trajectories and export Alpaca / ShareGPT instruction-tuning datasets: ```python from core.evolution.dataset_curator import get_dataset_curator curator = get_dataset_curator() # Export curated training dataset curator.export_dataset("~/.locus/evolution/dataset_alpaca.jsonl", min_reward=0.7) # Generate 4-bit Unsloth QLoRA training script curator.generate_qlora_training_config("~/.locus/evolution/dataset_alpaca.jsonl", output_script_path="train_qlora.py") ``` --- ## 6. 🧪 Run the Complete 35-Test Automated Verification Suite Run the master test suite across all 6 phases: ```bash python -m pytest core/test_core.py core/test_memory_backends.py core/test_phase3.py core/test_phase4.py core/test_phase5.py core/test_phase6.py -v ``` --- ## 7. 🐳 Multi-Container Docker Production Stack To run the full production multi-container environment (Locus Main, FastAPI Sidecar on `:8420`, Neo4j Community on `:7687`, PostgreSQL pgvector on `:5432`, and Redis on `:6379`): ```bash docker compose up -d ```

🤖 Assistant 2026-08-31T14:34:55Z
# ⚡ Locus OS Installer (Pi Agent Style) We have created cross-platform, one-command install scripts for **Locus OS** just like **Pi Agent**: --- ## 🪟 Windows (PowerShell) Open PowerShell in the project directory and run: ```powershell .\install.ps1 ``` > **One-Liner (Remote / Web Style)**: > ```powershell > irm https://raw.githubusercontent.com/Shivamxp23/locus-os/main/install.ps1 | iex > ``` --- ## 🪟 Windows (Command Prompt / Double-Click) Run `install.bat` directly from CMD or double-click it in File Explorer: ```cmd install.bat ``` --- ## 🐧 Linux & 🍎 macOS (Bash) Make executable and run: ```bash chmod +x install.sh ./install.sh ``` > **One-Liner (Remote / Web Style)**: > ```bash > curl -fsSL https://raw.githubusercontent.com/Shivamxp23/locus-os/main/install.sh | bash > ``` --- ### 🔧 What the Installer Does Automatically: 1. **Python Check**: Verifies Python 3.10+ installation. 2. **Workspace Setup**: Creates and initializes `~/.locus/` (`vault/`, `headroom_cache/`, `evolution/`, `diagnostics/`). 3. **Package Linking**: Fast-links the `locus` package into your environment. 4. **PATH Configuration**: Registers the native `locus` executable in your user PATH. 5. **Initial Doctor Probe**: Runs `locus doctor` to verify all subsystems are healthy.

1: # Development Rules
2: 
3: ## Conversational Style
4: 
5: - Keep answers short and concise
6: - No emojis in commits, issues, PR comments, or code
7: - No fluff or cheerful filler text (e.g., "Thanks @user" not "Thanks so much @user!")
8: - Technical prose only, be direct
9: - When the user asks a question, answer it first before making edits or running implementation commands.
10: - When responding to user feedback or an analysis, explicitly say whether you agree or disagree before saying what you changed.
11: 
12: ## Code Quality
13: 
14: - Read files in full before wide-ranging changes, before editing files you have not fully inspected, and when asked to investigate or audit. Do not rely on search snippets for broad changes.
15: - No `any` unless absolutely necessary.
16: - Inline single-line helpers that have only one call site.
17: - Check node_modules for external API types; don't guess.
18: - **No inline imports** (`await import()`, `import("pkg").Type`, dynamic type imports). Top-level imports only.
19: - Never remove or downgrade code to fix type errors from outdated deps; upgrade the dep instead.
20: - Use only erasable TypeScript syntax (Node strip-only mode) in code checked by the root config (`packages/*/src`, `packages/*/test`, `packages/coding-agent/examples`): no parameter properties, `enum`, `namespace`/`module`, `import =`, `export =`, or other constructs needing JS emit. Use explicit fields with constructor assignments.

3: This guide exists to save both sides time.

5: ## The One Rule
6: 
7: **You must understand your code.** If you cannot explain what your changes do and how they interact with the rest of the system, your PR will be closed.
8:

13: ## Contribution Gate
14: 
15: All issues and PRs from new contributors are auto-closed by default.
16: 
17: Issues submitted Friday through Sunday are not reviewed. If something is urgent, ask on Discord: https://discord.com/invite/3cU7Bz4UPx
18: 
19: Maintainers review auto-closed issues daily and reopen worthwhile ones. Issues that do not meet the quality bar below will not be reopened or receive a reply.
20: 
21: Approval happens through maintainer replies on issues:
22: 
23: - `lgtmi`: your future issues will not be auto-closed
24: - `lgtm`: your future issues and PRs will not be auto-closed
25: 
26: `lgtmi` does not grant rights to submit PRs. Only `lgtm` grants rights to submit PRs.
27: 
28: ## Quality Bar For Issues
29: 
30: If you open an issue, you must use one of the two GitHub issue templates.

1: # Security Policy
2: 
3: This document should guide you about understanding the security concept behind
4: Pi and also where the boundaries are.
5: 
6: In general Pi is a coding agent that runs locally within the security boundary
7: of the user that is running it.  It's the responsibiltiy of the user to monitor
8: its operations or to contain it within a container, virtual machine or other
9: Sandbox solution.
10: 
11: Pi treats the local user account and files writable by that account as inside
12: the same trust boundary as the Pi process itself.  If an attacker can modify files
13: under the user's home directory, workspace, shell startup files, environment, or
14: Pi configuration, they can generally influence Pi or other local developer tools.
15: Reports that depend on such prior local write access are not security
16: vulnerabilities unless they demonstrate how Pi grants that write access or crosses
17: an operating-system privilege boundary.
18: 
19: Pi relies on users installing trustworthy extensions and loading trustworthy
20: skills and only to use pi within trusted repositories.  This is because files
21: like `AGENTS.md` or instructions in comments can be used to prompt inject the
22: coding agent trivially and this cannot be protected against.
23: 
24: ## Reporting a Vulnerability
25: 
26: If you believe you found a security vulnerability in pi or another package in
27: this repository, please report it privately by either:
28: 
29: - Emailing `security@earendil.co

🤖 Assistant 2026-08-31T21:01:52Z
# 🚀 How to Launch the Locus CLI You can launch the Locus CLI directly from any terminal (Command Prompt, PowerShell, or Windows Terminal) using the `locus` command: --- ## 1. 🖥️ Launch the Real-Time Terminal Dashboard This opens the live 4-quadrant interactive TUI dashboard displaying your real-time **Daily Capacity Score (DCS)** gauge, **4-Faction balance meters** (Craft, Health, Leverage, Expression), active **Task Queue**, and background telemetry stream: ```cmd locus dashboard ``` > **Tip**: If you just want to print a single static frame to the console and exit: > ```cmd > locus dashboard --once > ``` --- ## 2. 🩺 Run Subsystem Diagnostics & Health Probe Inspect connectivity and operational status across the state bus, Obsidian vault, Master Conductor, and Headroom CCR cache: ```cmd locus doctor ``` --- ## 3. ⚙️ View and Manage Configuration ```cmd # View current config locus config show # Check for missing required config variables locus config check # View config file path locus config path ``` --- ## 4. 🚀 Launch the Messaging Gateway Start the live messaging bridge that connects incoming Telegram requests into the 11-step Master Conductor: ```cmd locus gateway run -v ``` --- ## 5. 🛠️ Run the Setup Wizard ```cmd locus setup ``` --- ## 💡 Fallback Direct Python Execution If you are in a shell where PATH hasn't refreshed yet, you can also run any command directly with Python from the `locus-os` directory: ```bash python -m locus_cli.main dashboard python -m locus_cli.main doctor python -m locus_cli.main --help ```

+def _cmd_single_prompt(prompt: str) -> None:
+    """Execute a single turn through the Master Conductor and print result."""
+    from core.conductor import get_conductor
+    conductor = get_conductor()
+    res = conductor.conduct(prompt, channel_type="cli")
+    response = res.get("response", "") if isinstance(res, dict) else str(res)
+    print(response)

# 🌌 Locus CLI: Built Directly Upon Pi Agent Architecture As specified in [`LOCUS_FEATURE_LOGIC_DECOMPOSITION.md`](file:///c:/Users/soni8/Desktop/everything/University%202.0/Project(s)/5_Locus/locus-os/.keysndocs/Documentation/Implementation%20Plans/LOCUS_FEATURE_LOGIC_DECOMPOSITION.md) (§1.1–1.6, §3.1–3.7, §7.1–7.5, §8.1), **Locus OS is designed as an interactive ambient and coding agent CLI that inherits, augments, and elevates Pi Agent's core architecture**: ``` ┌────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐ │ LOCUS OS CLI ARCHITECTURE │ ├────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┤ │ 1. Interactive Agent REPL │ Live conversation loop (locus > ) │ │ 2. Master Conductor (11-S) │ 6-Layer Context Forge + 17 Locus Tools │ │ 3. Terminal TUI Dashboard │ Live DCS Gauge, 4-Faction Progress Bars │ │ 4. Coding Agent Engine │ TypeScript interactive harness │ │ 5. Subsystems & Services │ Doctor, Gateway, Config, Deploy, Setup │ └────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘ ``` --- ## 💻 How to Run Locus Now ### 1. 🤖 Interactive Agent Mode (Default — Just Like Pi Agent) Simply typing `locus` in any Command Prompt or PowerShell terminal now **immediately launches the interactive conversational agent REPL**: ```cmd locus ``` ``` _ ____ ____ _ _ ____ ___ ____ | | / __ \ / ___| | | / ___| / _ \/ ___| | | | | | | | | | | \___ \ | | | \___ \ | |___| |__| | |___| |_| |___) | | |_| |___) | |_____|\____/ \____|\___/|____/ \___/|____/ 🌌 Locus OS — Ambient AI Operating System (Interactive Mode) Type your request or use slash commands (/dashboard, /doctor, /checkin, /help, /exit) locus > ``` Within the interactive session, you can: - Chat naturally and ask Locus to manage goals, search memory, or execute tasks. - Use slash commands like `/dashboard` (to see your live DCS), `/doctor` (to test subsystems), `/checkin`, `/clear`, `/help`, and `/exit`. --- ### 2. ⚡ Single-Turn Prompt Execution Run a prompt directly from the command line without entering the interactive loop: ```cmd locus -p "What is my current DCS and active schedule?" ``` --- ### 3. 🖥️ Real-Time 4-Quadrant Terminal Dashboard ```cmd locus dashboard ``` --- ### 4. 🩺 Subsystem Health & Doctor Probe ```cmd locus doctor ``` --- ### 5. 🧑‍💻 TypeScript Coding Agent Harness For dedicated multi-file codebase editing, bash executions, and tools (`packages/coding-agent`): ```bash locus-code # or: npx tsx packages/coding-agent/src/cli.ts ```
